The free plan also allows for up to 50,000 monthly active users and unlimited authentication and Cloud Firestore usage. Additionally, Firebase offers a range of paid plans with higher limits and additional features for users who require more capacity or functionality. The Flame plan is a fixed-rate upgrade plan offered by Firebase, designed to provide users with increased capacity and features beyond the free plan. Here are some details about the Flame plan:

Pricing: The Flame plan costs $25/month, which provides a fixed amount of usage for each resource. Additional usage beyond the fixed amount is billed at a per-usage rate.

Resources: The Flame plan includes increased resource limits compared to the free plan, including 20 GB of data storage, 200 GB of hosting bandwidth per month, and 2,000,000 daily function invocations.

Additional features: The Flame plan also includes additional features not available in the free plan, such as custom domain hosting, advanced security controls, and access to Firebase support.

Scalability: The Flame plan is designed to be scalable, allowing users to easily upgrade or downgrade their plan as their needs change. Users can also set spending limits and receive alerts when they approach their limits.

Overall, the Flame plan offers increased capacity and features for users who require more than the free plan provides, but who do not need the comprehensive feature set of the Firebase Plus plan. as hash is an hexadecimal number of 16 length
we can calculate it as: *16^(16)*
= *1.8446744e+19*
In English:
*Eighteen quintillion, four hundred and forty-six quadrillion, seven hundred and forty four trillion, seventy three billion, seven hundred and nine million, five hundred and fifty one thousand, six hundred and sixteen.*
In this way , every wallet can have unique wallet address
A never ending finite combinations of these wallet address I wrote this for fast students Who were saying how , every wallet will be given a unique address Ya ap na check Kia ha kahi sa Sir we studied , how to find combinations I also verified it from different sources (Number base )^(number of digits)

Gives you a total different combinations can be from this number null Sorry phir bt kt gae Apki awaz kat rahi thi It's ok Sir ya mana official send kia tha Aoa Zeshan AJ class krwa dna ma Zara dr ka pass ja rha Hu tu mja latte Hu jao Ok Sir aj kia prhana vo share kr da Kindly Enum struct loops array Ok NxT yahi chza ma nft ma ft na dobra repeat krwa do ga null Ma Baki b Karta Hu share Ma sir updated share krta pr complete nhi hoe abhi Hardhat tk done ha

With title : *Third Day of my periods* Sir ya koi bt ha public ma krna ki Aoa W salam sie Sir No G bhtr Sir ma koshish bohat kr raha lms ma koch rh na jay Jis tara fahad kh raha Agay Allah bhtr kara 🤲 👍 Sir  , sir ahmad manzoor ko bi eid mubrarik kah dijuya ga hamari taraf sa Kia musrifayt ha Eid Ki Shukria g Inshallah zror Sir ap batay na ap kasa mutmayin Jasa ap mutmayin ho wasa kerta Ma Har tra Sa mumtin hu Ahmad ka muja message aya ha Ma akala ajaya no issue Ma indrive kr kh a jata then vaha sa la jata No issue I am coming Done Abhi ride mili bs a raha Done Ajia Slides I want to create a web platform where customers can purchase QR codes that link to personalized memorial pages. These pages will allow users to upload videos, texts, and images, and others can view and leave comments. Admins will approve comments before they are visible.

**Key Features:**

1. **User Registration and Login:**
- Users can create accounts, log in, and manage their profiles.

2. **QR Code Generation:**
- After purchasing a QR code through Shopify, a unique QR code and URL are generated for each user.

3. **Memorial Page Creation:**
- Users can create a memorial page, upload videos, texts, and images.
- The memorial page URL is linked to the QR code.

4. **Comment System:**
- Visitors can leave comments on the memorial pages.
- Comments require admin approval before they are published.

5. **Admin Dashboard:**
- Admins can manage user accounts, approve comments, and monitor activities.

**Technical Requirements:**

1. **Frontend:**
- HTML, CSS, JavaScript (possibly React.js or Vue.js).

2. **Backend:**
- Node.js with Express.js or another suitable framework.
- MongoDB for database management.

3. **QR Code Generation:**
- Use a library like `qrcode` for generating QR codes.

4. **Integration with Shopify:**
- Use Shopify API to manage sales and order fulfillment.

5. **Hosting:**
- Deploy the application on platforms like AWS, Heroku, or Vercel.

**Detailed Workflow:**

1. **Shopify Integration:**
- Customers purchase a QR code product.
- Once purchased, trigger a webhook to generate a QR code and create a user account.

2. **User Dashboard:**
- Users log in to their dashboard to create and manage their memorial page.
- They can upload videos, texts, and images.

3. **QR Code and Memorial Page:**
- Generated QR code links to the user’s unique memorial page URL.
- Display the QR code and link in the user dashboard.

4. **Comment System:**
- Visitors can leave comments which are stored in the database.
- Admins review and approve comments via the admin dashboard before they appear on the site.

5. **Admin Dashboard:**
- Admins log in to view all users and their memorial pages.
- Admins can approve or delete comments.

**Security:**
- Ensure data security and user authentication.
- Implement measures to protect against spam and malicious content in comments.

**Tools and Technologies:**
- **Frontend:** React.js, Vue.js, or plain HTML/CSS/JavaScript.
- **Backend:** Node.js, Express.js.
- **Database:** MongoDB.
- **QR Code Generation:** `qrcode` library.
- **Shopify Integration:** Shopify API for handling orders and payments.
- **Hosting:** AWS, Heroku, or Vercel. Zeshan ya dkha ktna pages bna ga Max 19-25 Pages 4.5 tak Isa Sa budget Kia kahyal ha Sara Hamara sir ya center ma koi middle man ha? Jun like 5 to 6 month bnta h Aoa Zeshan telecom ma dkha ka kn kn Sa projects AI ma Hu rha ha Ya Hu skta ha Ma b search KR rha Hu rat tak companies ma proposal DNA ha ANZ na Ok sir ma dakhta Ghar ja kr Ok done 1) *AI is being used to analyze data from network sensors to identify potential problems before they occur. This allows telecom providers to take proactive steps to fix problems and prevent outages. For example, companies are using AI to predict network congestion and proactively reroute traffic to avoid outages*

2) Traffic Management: AI algorithms can analyze network traffic patterns in real-time to optimize routing and manage congestion.

3) Chatbots and Virtual Assistants: AI-powered chatbots can handle customer inquiries, provide support, and troubleshoot common issues, enhancing customer service availability and responsiveness.

4) Anomaly Detection: AI can identify unusual patterns in network usage that may indicate fraudulent activities or security breaches, enabling quicker response and mitigation.

5) Churn Prediction: AI can analyze customer behavior to predict churn, enabling telecom companies to take proactive measures to retain customers. import * as Device from "expo-device";
import * as Notification from "expo-notifications";
import Constants from "expo-constants";
import { Platform } from "react-native";
import { router } from "expo-router";
import * as TaskManager from "expo-task-manager";

export const usePushNotifications = () => {
Notification.setNotificationHandler({
handleNotification: async () => ({
shouldPlaySound: true,
shouldShowAlert: true,
shouldSetBadge: true,
}),
});

const [expoPushToken, setExpoPushToken] = useState();
const [notification, setNotification] = useState();

const notificationListener = useRef();
const responseListener = useRef();

async function registerForPushNotificationsAsync() {
let token;

if (Device.isDevice) {
const { status: existingStatus } =
await Notification.getPermissionsAsync();

let finalStatus = existingStatus;

if (existingStatus !== "granted") {
const { status } = await Notification.requestPermissionsAsync();
finalStatus = status;
}
if (finalStatus !== "granted") {
alert("Failed to get push token");
}

token = await Notification.getExpoPushTokenAsync({
projectId: Constants.easConfig?.extra?.eas?.projectId,
});

if (Platform.OS == "android") {
try {
await Notification.setNotificationChannelAsync("default", {
name: "default",
importance: Notification.AndroidImportance.MAX,
vibrationPattern: [500, 500, 500, 500, 500],
lightColor: "#7A2715",
enableLights: true,
enableVibrate: true,
bypassDnd: true,
showBadge: true,
sound: "default",
description:
"It is Push notification\n(Vibrate might be limited by device settings)",
lockscreenVisibility:
Notification.AndroidNotificationVisibility.PUBLIC,
});

await Notification.setNotificationChannelAsync("priority", {
name: "priority",
importance: Notification.AndroidImportance.MAX,
vibrationPattern: [500, 500, 500, 500, 500, 500],
lightColor: "#7A2715",
enableLights: true,
enableVibrate: true,
bypassDnd: true,
showBadge: true,
sound: "default",
audioAttributes: {
flags: {
requestHardwareAudioVideoSynchronization: true,
enforceAudibility: true,
},
},
description:
"It is Push notification\n(Vibrate might be limited by device settings)",
lockscreenVisibility:
Notification.AndroidNotificationVisibility.PUBLIC,
});
} catch (err) {
console.log(err);
}
}

return token;
} else {
alert("Physical Device is required");
}
}

// Define the background notification handler task
TaskManager.defineTask("BACKGROUND_NOTIFICATION_TASK", ({ data, error }) => {
if (error) {
console.error("Background notification task error:", error);
return;
}
// Handle your background notification
console.log("Received background notification:", data);

// You can show a local notification here if needed
Notification.scheduleNotificationAsync({
content: {
title: "Background Notification",
body: "You have a new notification.",
sound: "default",
},
trigger: null,
});
});

// Register the task
Notification.registerTaskAsync("BACKGROUND_NOTIFICATION_TASK");

useEffect(() => {
registerForPushNotificationsAsync().then((token) => {
setExpoPushToken(token);
});

notificationListener.current = Notification.addNotificationReceivedListener(
(notification) => {
// setNotification(notification);
const url = notification.request.content.data?.url;
// router.replace(url);
router.navigate(url);
}
);

responseListener.current =
Notification.addNotificationResponseReceivedListener((response) => {
console.log(response);
});
return () => {
// Notification.removeNotificationSubscription(notificationListener.current);
// Notification.removeNotificationSubscription(responseListener.current);
};
}, []);

return { expoPushToken, notification };
}; Show in foreground Kaha ha The push notification is working well like (defualt sound and vibration , comes on the screen on high prioritiy)

Plan kia ha sb chza step by step shre krta hu prev experience sa or Baki soucha ha bht c chza 👍 👍 👍 Bs tura time ha Sahi time or moka ka wait ha 👍 G kr da import express from "express"
import cors from "cors"
import { nanoid } from 'nanoid'


const app = express();
app.use(express.json());
app.use(cors());


const port = process.env.PORT || 3000;


let userBase = []; // TODO: replace this with mongoDB

app.post("/signup", (req, res) => {

let body = req.body;

if (!body.firstName
|| !body.lastName
|| !body.email
|| !body.password
) {
res.status(400).send(
`required fields missing, request example:
{
"firstName": "John",
"lastName": "Doe",
"password": "12345"
}`
);
return;
}

let isFound = false;

for (let i = 0; i < userBase.length; i++) {
if (userBase[i].email === body.email.toLowerCase()) {
isFound = true;
break;
}
}
if (isFound) { // this email already exist
res.status(400).send({
message: `email ${body.email} already exist.`
});
return;
}


let newUser = {
userId: nanoid(),
firstName: body.firstName,
lastName: body.lastName,
email: body.email.toLowerCase(),
password: body.password
}

userBase.push(newUser);

res.status(201).send({ message: "user is created" });
});

app.post("/login", (req, res) => {

let body = req.body;

if (!body.email || !body.password) { // null check - undefined, "", 0 , false, null , NaN
res.status(400).send(
`required fields missing, request example:
{
"password": "12345"
}`
);
return;
}


for (let i = 0; i < userBase.length; i++) {
if (userBase[i].email === body.email) {

isFound = true;
if (userBase[i].password === body.password) { // correct password

res.status(200).send({
firstName: userBase[i].firstName,
lastName: userBase[i].lastName,
email: userBase[i].email,
message: "login successful",
token: "some unique token"
})
return;

} else { // password incorrect

res.status(401).send({
message: "incorrect password"
})
return;
}
}
}

if (!isFound) {
res.status(404).send({
message: "user not found"
})
return;
}
})



app.listen(port, () => {
console.log(`Example app listening on port ${port}`)
}) <!DOCTYPE html>

<input type="text" id="lastName" required>
<br>

<label for="email">Email</label>
<input type="email" id="email" required>
<br>

<label for="password">Password</label>
<input type="password" id="password" required>
<br>

<label for="repeatPassword">Repeat Password</label>
<input type="password" id="repeatPassword" required>
<br>

<input type="submit" value="Signup">
</form>

<br>

<script>

function signup() {

var firstName = document.getElementById('firstName').value;
var lastName = document.getElementById('lastName').value;
var email = document.getElementById('email').value;
var password = document.getElementById('password').value;
var repeatPassword = document.getElementById('repeatPassword').value;

if (password !== repeatPassword) {
document.querySelector("#message").innerHTML = 'Passwords do not match, please try again';
return;
}


firstName,
lastName,
email,
password,

})
.then(function (response) {
console.log(response.data);
document.querySelector("#message").innerHTML = response.data.message;
})
.catch(function (error) {
console.log(error.response.data);
document.querySelector("#message").innerHTML = error.response.data.message;
});
}



</script>


</body>

Description
I'm looking for a skilled developer than can create an AI software for voice modulation and cloning. It will need to be an isolated system capable of turning speech to speech using AI voice. Will need to be able to create and clone voices ourselves and use these freely. Additionally the AI should be capable of cloning voices in real-time.
It is preferred for this to be an application that can be used locally and not required to be stored as a cloud-service or attached to a pre-existing AI system or bot.
Compensation:
3-6 months


Seeing this message means you are very very closer to finding meaningful work and increasing your earning potential.
I am committed to working with you on this project. '
However, before we commence, I would like to ensure that you are the ideal candidate for the role. Please provide the following details:

Strategy Overview: A step-by-step explanation of your planned approach to the project.
- Previous Experience: Details of your relevant past projects and achievements.
- Timeline and Budget: While I intend to adhere to the listed budget and timeline, I would appreciate a more detailed outline of the project milestones and the anticipated completion dates to better understand the project's progression.
- Project Report: A comprehensive document that includes:
Project timeline.
Budget estimates.
Detailed explanation of each step involved in the project.

The clarity and detail in your explanation will be essential in demonstrating your suitability for this position.
Please submit the report within 1 hour. I kindly request that you refrain from asking questions until I have had the opportunity to respond, as I am currently occupied.

Thank you for your understanding.

Best regards. Wilbert Ya dkha zra ma b dkh rha hu GPT Gg isa dkha zra is ma ham kia kia use kra tu ya asa build hu skta h ma woi chza souch rha hu 1. Initial Research & Planning
Voice Modulation and Cloning Technology:
Study state-of-the-art voice synthesis models like Tacotron 2, WaveNet, and FastSpeech, which are used for realistic speech synthesis.
Research existing models (e.g., DeepVoice or VITS) that are capable of cloning voices based on short audio samples and use these as a baseline for customization.
Determine the best deep learning frameworks to use, such as TensorFlow, PyTorch, or specialized libraries like ESPnet for speech synthesis.
2. Data Collection & Training Pipeline
Voice Dataset:
Collect or generate a dataset of voice samples from various speakers. The dataset should include both male and female voices and a variety of tones and accents.
Implement a data augmentation pipeline (such as adding noise, pitch changes) to make the AI more robust.
Voice Cloning:
Develop a module to clone voices by training the AI model using a small number of voice samples.
Use few-shot learning techniques so that the AI can learn to mimic voices with minimal data input.
Fine-tune models using transfer learning to handle various voice styles and accents.
Speech-to-Speech Modulation:
Integrate a voice modulation system that transforms a source voice into the cloned voice in real-time.
Use advanced techniques like Mel-spectrogram generation and pitch shifting to ensure accurate modulation.
3. Real-time Voice Processing
Latency Optimization:
Develop a real-time processing engine using GPU acceleration for faster inference with minimal delay.
Implement low-latency neural vocoders (such as HiFi-GAN) for converting generated speech representations back into waveform audio in real-time.
Audio Input & Output:
Implement a microphone input module for capturing live voice input and processing it through the AI model.
Create an efficient audio output pipeline for delivering the synthesized voice over various interfaces (local audio output, streaming platforms, etc.).
4. Local Deployment and Isolation
Standalone Application:
Build a standalone desktop application (using frameworks like Electron, C++ with Qt, or Python with Tkinter).
Ensure the system runs entirely locally, with no cloud dependencies, so that users can clone and use voices offline.
Voice Cloning Interface:
Design an easy-to-use GUI where users can upload voice samples for cloning and interact with real-time modulation settings.
Incorporate options for users to store, manage, and modify cloned voices.
5. Testing and Validation
Conduct testing to ensure the AI model produces high-quality, natural voice outputs.
Test real-time cloning to ensure there’s minimal distortion or latency in voice modulation during live interactions.
Validate the system’s ability to clone different types of voices (gender, accent) with accuracy.
6. Security & Privacy
Ensure that the system is secure and that voice data is handled locally to respect user privacy.
Implement voice data encryption to avoid potential exploitation or misuse of cloned voices.
7. User Experience and Final Tuning
User feedback loop to allow users to tweak and refine the quality of the cloned voices.
Perform final fine-tuning based on user experience, ensuring that the application is intuitive, fast, and reliable.
Previous Experience:
I have worked on similar projects involving AI-based voice synthesis, including real-time audio processing and AI training pipelines. My expertise includes:

Implementing machine learning models for speech synthesis.
Developing real-time processing systems for audio applications using CUDA and GPU acceleration.
Building offline, standalone applications that run without cloud dependencies.
+ Ok Ya dkha zra kuch mila Client man rha ha payment pa tu Goolgle Pricing zyada ha thori Kia ha ok sun lia sir Muhammad Zeeshan Web Collberation.vcf (file attached) Audii msj send nai hu rha Just txt ja rha h ok ok Aj koi jlsa etc h gg pti Asa bagrt h ka apna chkro ma sb ka nuksan gg Phases & Technologies:

Research & Model Selection (2 weeks)

Tech: TensorFlow, PyTorch
Cloud: Pre-trained models via AWS Sagemaker/Google Cloud AI
Reason: Efficient model selection ensures quality results while using cloud services alleviates the need for heavy local computing resources.

Voice Cloning & Training (4 weeks)

Tech: DeepVoice, VITS, Mel-spectrogram generation
Cloud: Custom training on AWS/Google Cloud with additional pre-built APIs for voice cloning
Reason: Utilizing pre-built APIs speeds up the cloning process, while custom training allows us to fine-tune models for our specific requirements.

Real-time Modulation (4 weeks)

Tech: HiFi-GAN, CUDA
Cloud: AWS EC2 for GPU acceleration
Reason: Low-latency, real-time voice processing requires robust cloud infrastructure for efficient model deployment and performance.

Mobile App Development (3 weeks)

Tech: React Native for cross-platform mobile application
Reason: Enables deployment on both iOS and Android with a single codebase, ensuring a native-like user experience.

Web App Development (3 weeks)

Tech: Next.js for server-side rendering and SEO optimization
Reason: Offers fast performance and allows for easy integration with APIs for voice processing.

Desktop App Development (3 weeks)

Tech: Electron.js for a cross-platform desktop application
Reason: Ensures a consistent user experience across Windows, macOS, and Linux while maintaining offline functionality.

Testing & Fine-Tuning (4 weeks)

Tech: PyTest, real-time validation
Reason: Comprehensive testing is essential to ensure the system’s accuracy and performance in real-world applications.

Why Use Cloud:
Leveraging cloud services for model training and APIs allows us to utilize powerful computing resources, expedite training, and streamline the deployment process, while ensuring the final application remains locally accessible for users.

Timeline:
5-6 months is required for model training, real-time optimization, and thorough testing to ensure accuracy and performance.

Best regards,

Sir React Native Overall JS hi ha

for Native Modules

ap poch raha to
Android ma java ma likhta lakin kotlin bi supported ha
Ios ma Objective - C or Swift bi supported Multitherding ya back pa kuch task perform krna hu ma us sens ma bat ki ha Sir, vo native modules likhna pra ga na Tu hama kotlin lkhna best hu ge ya java Or iOS ka lia swift dkha ga ya objective c Us sens ma hama kuch language preference dkhni ha Smj i bat Gg us sense ma sir

Phir Kotlin

or udhr swift

keouki moder suntax and community support  , java or objective - c ma wasa updates nhi Ref:
In Summary:
Kotlin is the preferred choice for writing new native modules in React Native due to its modern syntax, ease of use, and better long-term support in Android development.
Java is still fully supported and might be a good choice if you're maintaining legacy code or have existing Java-based infrastructure. Ya SB jago sa ma b asa hi guzra hu G sir Sir , supply chain ma bi bohat use hota Gg Fine G barish ho rahi The MERN Bootcamp for Beginners is a 10-week program designed for individuals who are new to Web Development and want to learn how to build dynamic Web Applications using the MERN (MongoDB, Express.js, React.js, and Node.js) stack. The program includes 120+ hours of instruction over 5 days a week, with each session lasting 3 hours.

*Why MERN Development?*

1. High Demand:
The MERN stack (MongoDB, Express, React, and Node.js) is one of the most in-demand technology stacks in the web development industry. According to Stack Overflow, the demand for the MERN stack has increased by 74% over the last five years.

2. Lucrative Career:
MERN developers are highly sought after and are paid well in the industry. The average salary of a MERN developer is around PKR - 150,000 per month in the Pakistan, according to Glassdoor.

3. Versatility:
With MERN, you can build full-stack web applications that can be used across different platforms, including desktop and mobile. This means that you can create a wide range of web applications for various industries.

4. Open-source community:
MERN stack is based on open-source technologies, which means that there are plenty of resources available online for developers to learn and contribute to the community.

*Why choose Game Train?*
1. Industry-tailored training programs
2. Certified trainers
3. 90% hiring rate
4. Mentorship from industry experts
5. Additional Prep Resources (Resume, Cover Letter, Interview etc)

*Learning Objectives:*
1. Understand the fundamentals of web development and the MERN stack (HTML, CSS, Tailwind CSS).
2. Explore advanced JavaScript topics Asynchronous programming, closures, promises, async/await and ES6 features like Destructuring, spread/rest operators, and arrow functions, IFEE, Callback Functions.
3. Setup a development environment and work with Git and GitHub
4. Build and manage MongoDB databases Create RESTful APIs using Express.js
5. Build interactive user interfaces with React.js
6. Develop web servers with Node.js
7. Integrate MongoDB, Express.js, React.js, and Node.js to build full-stack applications
8. Implement authentication and authorization in web applications
9. Deploy MERN applications to production servers

*Duration & Format:*
1. Classes (5-Days a Week) (Monday to Friday)
2. Morning: 9:30 am - 12:30 pm
3. Remote & Onsite Classes
4. 10 Weeks

*Fees & Mode of Payment:*
- PKR 20,000 (Nationwide)
- *Scholarship: 34%*
- Fee after scholarship: 30,000 - 10,000 = *20,000*

*Instalment Payment Procedure:*
Installments: Students can pay the fee in multiple instalments throughout the duration of the boot camp:
- Total fee = *PKR. 20,000*
- 5,000 - Seat Reservation
- 10,000 - 15 Days
- 5,000 - 15 Days

Kia use krna ha hum na G ok Ahmad Hassan sa pucha wn next js ka stah react native wl bnda b in kra cv I hu ge or UNa lagya Ham ai wli side dkhta ha G ok Fittens or health ka according bari zbradst product ya Nikal sakti ha hamri G ok , krta ma zara dakh rha Building a React Native app that opens the camera, captures a picture of an object (e.g., food), and then tells the user how many calories are in it involves a few distinct parts. Here’s an overview of the tech stack and architecture you might consider:


---

1. Mobile Framework & UI

React Native
Use React Native as your core framework. If you want an even simpler setup, consider using Expo, which offers streamlined configuration and built-in support for common functionalities (including the camera).

Camera Integration

React Native Camera or Expo Camera: These libraries let you access the device’s camera and capture images.

Expo Camera is very straightforward if you’re using Expo.




---

2. Image Recognition (Food Identification)

Since you need to recognize what food is in the picture before calculating calories, you have two primary approaches:

On-Device (Edge) Processing:

TensorFlow Lite / TensorFlow.js

You can integrate a pre-trained food classification model (like a converted Food-101 model) to run locally on the device.

Pros: Works offline, reduces latency.

Cons: Increases app size, may require more device resources, and the model’s accuracy might be limited depending on your implementation.



Cloud-Based Image Recognition API:

Google Cloud Vision API, Clarifai, or AWS Rekognition

These APIs can analyze the image and return a prediction of the food item.

Pros: Likely higher accuracy, less processing on the device.

Cons: Requires network connectivity and careful handling of user data/privacy.



Note: Large Language Models (LLMs) like GPT are great for text-based tasks but are not designed for image recognition. For detecting food items in images, a specialized image recognition API or model is a better fit.


---

3. Calorie/Nutrition Data Retrieval

Once you’ve identified the food item, you need to determine its calorie content. Consider these options:

Nutrition APIs:

Edamam Nutrition Analysis API, USDA FoodData Central API, or Nutritionix API can provide detailed nutritional information, including calorie counts.

The flow would be: the image recognition step returns a food item name → then your app (or backend) queries a nutrition API to retrieve the calorie info.


Local Database (if applicable):

For a more offline-friendly approach, you might bundle a small, static nutritional database with your app. However, this may require significant effort to keep updated and accurate.




---

4. Backend (Optional, but Recommended for Cloud Processing)

If you choose to use cloud-based services for image recognition or to handle API keys securely:

Node.js/Express or Python (Flask/Django):

You can create a simple REST API that the mobile app communicates with.

This backend can handle calls to the image recognition API and the nutrition database API, then return the combined result to the app.

Benefits: Keeps sensitive API keys off the client and allows you to process and merge data before sending it back to the mobile app.




---

5. Putting It All Together

1. User Interaction:
The user opens the app and uses the camera (via React Native Camera or Expo Camera) to take a picture.


2. Image Processing:
The app either:

Sends the image to your backend, which then calls a cloud image recognition API (e.g., Google Cloud Vision) to identify the food, or

Processes the image on-device using TensorFlow Lite/TensorFlow.js.



3. Food Identification:
The API/model returns the identified food item.


4. Calorie Lookup:
With the food item name, your app or backend then queries a nutrition API (like Edamam or USDA FoodData Central) to fetch the calorie information.


5. Display Results:
The calorie information is sent back to the React Native app, which displays it to the user.




---

Summary of Recommended Tech Stack

Frontend/Mobile App:

React Native (or Expo for simpler setup)

Camera Integration: Expo Camera or React Native Camera


Image Recognition:

Cloud-based: Google Cloud Vision API / Clarifai / AWS Rekognition

Or On-Device: TensorFlow Lite / TensorFlow.js (with a food classification model)


Nutrition Data:

Nutrition APIs (e.g., Edamam, USDA FoodData Central, Nutritionix)


Backend (Optional but advised):

Server Framework: Node.js/Express or Python Flask/Django

Purpose: Securely handle API keys, process images, and merge data from different services




---

Final Thoughts

Cloud vs. On-Device:
For better accuracy and less strain on the device, cloud-based image recognition is typically preferred. However, if offline functionality is crucial, on-device processing with TensorFlow Lite is an option—keeping in mind the challenges of model size and performance.

LLM API Use:
While LLMs are powerful for natural language processing, they are not designed for image recognition tasks. They could be used to generate user-friendly descriptions or additional insights once you have the food data, but the core tasks (object detection and calorie lookup) should rely on image recognition and nutrition APIs.


By following this stack, you’ll have a robust system that leverages specialized tools for each part of the workflow. Numan kahta sir ksi larki na hath cut kar lia tha apka picaha csr Bata rhi thi Fazol ksm ka afwha Hmmm hehe ap ka mureednia Ho skta hina ho hehe Wn tu hasid ha Hehe abu huraira sa pocha hina sorat ka hehe +92 302 9854114 numan ka is ka elwa wla num ha apka pass Aik din kia tha message usna ma nicha chat ma kahi dakhta Gg mil gya ha Ok sir Dakh kh sir batata Sir I am driving I will share it with you after one hour Overview of Captivate 360

Captivate 360 will be a comprehensive business software suite consisting of multiple products, covering essential business functions. The goal is to build a system that meets various business needs while ensuring scalability, integration, and reliability.

Core Products & Initial Focus

The platform will consist of several modules:

LMS (Learning Management System) – A platform for online learning, training, and course management.

BAT (Book a Trainer) – A system for scheduling and managing virtual courses.

HR Management – A module for handling human resources, employee records, and payroll.

CRM (Customer Relationship Management) – A dedicated CRM for managing client interactions and sales.

Additional Business Tools – Other software solutions to support various business operations in the future.


Initial Development Focus:

We will start by developing the LMS and BAT modules as part of our Minimum Viable Product (MVP).

Further development will be done incrementally based on market needs and feedback.


3. Key Development Principles

Fail-Safe System: The system must be highly reliable, with redundancy and backup mechanisms in place to prevent failures.

Modular & API-Driven Approach: The product should be designed in a way that allows businesses to integrate additional modules via APIs or other suitable methods.

White-Label Capability: Businesses should have the option to rebrand and customize the platform under their own brand identity.

Scalability & Flexibility: The system should be adaptable so that new products and features can be integrated easily. Yes, Greece does have some restrictions on the Play Store app, primarily related to gambling apps, where specific licensing and regulations are in place for online casino games, sports betting, daily fantasy sports, and lotteries; meaning not all gambling apps may be available in Greece depending on their compliance with local laws. ** Acha kahyal rkhta ha Web pa b ha Local law ban etc ka kuch Web nhi App ka ha seene ban ka Pwa etc wali game khal skta ha agr krni hu tu gg bilqul Aoa W salam Ma apko 5 min ma call krta hu null Ya 300 per student per month ha na Gg asa hi krna ha Phir fine ha 300/mo zaida kara ga tu 250/mon Fine ha Isa khna ha 1000 std ka lia ham na storage or Baki chza setup kr dta ha Gg G ok

Zeeshan Ali Aj hi aya tha or aj hi kia Saliha Message kr dia or asa message bhaj bi dia Ya dkha zra G tcs krvana ja rha a kh dakhta **As an AI expert, I currently see Zahanat AI as more of a marketing hype rather than a groundbreaking AI model—unless concrete technical proof, research papers, or real-world applications emerge.**

### **My Observations:**
1. **No Technical Documentation** – If this were a truly independent AI model, there would be research papers, technical documentation, or an open-source repository available to examine its development.

2. **No API or Public Testing** – Leading AI models like OpenAI’s GPT and Google’s Gemini provide public APIs and demo versions for testing. As of now, Zahanat AI has not made anything like that available.

3. **The Concept of ‘Culturally Aligned AI’ Is Not New** – Models like GPT, Claude, and Mistral can already be fine-tuned for different languages and cultural contexts. If Zahanat AI is promoting this as a unique feature, it is more of a **fine-tuning adjustment rather than an innovation.**

4. **Building a Truly Local AI Model Is Extremely Challenging** – Creating an AI model on the scale of GPT-4 requires **billions of dollars and access to high-end computing resources (TPUs, GPUs).** If Zahanat AI were developing its own model from scratch, it would need substantial infrastructure and investment.

5. **If It’s Just Focused on Urdu and Regional Languages?** – Even then, it's not groundbreaking because **GPT-4, Claude 3, and Mistral already support Urdu and several other regional languages.**

### **So, Is This a Real AI Model?**
**For now, it appears to be more of a branding campaign than an actual AI breakthrough.** The most likely scenario is that Zahanat AI is leveraging an existing large language model (such as GPT or Mistral) and rebranding it under its own name.

**If it truly is an independent AI model, the developers should release technical details, APIs, and public demos so that the community can test it.** Until then, I consider it more of a **hype than a reality.** Is ma dkha zra ham or kia add kr skta ha Kis lehaz sa Social media post ka lahz sa G ma prhta Dkha zra jn point lkha ha  wn or secodly Kal tak demo req pa wait b kr lata ha Muja to lgta bad ma ya picha sa Agentic RAG nikla ga null null Punjabi hehehe Hehehe Vo Fb Page ki access li thi vo remove kr di kia Fine null Class 11: AI Doctor – The Future of Medical AI
